2026-10-25T06:57:13.4305945Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4306318Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4306835Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4307079Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4307512Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4307839Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4308022Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4308844Z         """

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4309435Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4309830Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4310195Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4310539Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4310776Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4310981Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4311278Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4311507Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4311866Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4312111Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4326881Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4327150Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4466775Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4467172Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4467451Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4467706Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4468101Z     """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4468287Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4468433Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4468688Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4468880Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4469035Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4469327Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4469664Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4469841Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4470195Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4470455Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4470621Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4471049Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4472185Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4472430Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4472885Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4473393Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4473993Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4474454Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4474974Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4475411Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4475752Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4476206Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4476754Z             #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4477014Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4477434Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4477870Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4478294Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4478748Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479092Z             #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479447Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479780Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479984Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4480424Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4480867Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4481386Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4481651Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4481822Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4482151Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4482456Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4483019Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4483436Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4483789Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4484100Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4484481Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4484889Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4485332Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4485734Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4486093Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4486643Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487085Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487400Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487582Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487841Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4499939Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4500349Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4500597Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4500808Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4501170Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4501804Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4502226Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4502507Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4502787Z             log.debug(

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4503602Z                 options,
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4503858Z             )
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4504025Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4504294Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4504851Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4505449Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4508648Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4511307Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4517429Z             options,
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4517698Z         )
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4518157Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4518462Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4518615Z         # store configuration from user

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519221Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519384Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519727Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519980Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4520220Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4520674Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4520951Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4521184Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4521568Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4521846Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4522193Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4522511Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4522754Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4523057Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4529965Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4530365Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4530513Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4530821Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4531185Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4531490Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4531757Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4532114Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4532514Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4532820Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533013Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533156Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533445Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533877Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4534210Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4534454Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4534867Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535068Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535405Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535724Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535995Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4536269Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4536658Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4536853Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537116Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537348Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537723Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537997Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4538450Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4538855Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539034Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539181Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539415Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539600Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539793Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4540158Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4540597Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541023Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541277Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541501Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541997Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4542330Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4542669Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4542917Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543191Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543504Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543684Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543849Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4544375Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4544797Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4544988Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4545135Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4545443Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4545782Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4546080Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4546776Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547033Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547174Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547468Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547863Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4548206Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4548497Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4548756Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4549114Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4549495Z             return False

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659022Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659197Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659462Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659643Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4662623Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4662811Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4662952Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663256Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663396Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663560Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663770Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663986Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664143Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664214Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664429Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664585Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664762Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664940Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665136Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665281Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665463Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665608Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665741Z     """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665886Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666149Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666570Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666651Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666825Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667086Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667243Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667459Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667646Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667719Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667878Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668147Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668296Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668367Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668519Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668803Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668875Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669020Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669090Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669161Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669392Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669536Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669725Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669981Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670130Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670272Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670535Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670833Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670982Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4671055Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4671204Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4671280Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4677898Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678128Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678354Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678576Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678707Z #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678843Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679073Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679298Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679487Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679715Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679850Z #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679984Z set -eu
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680204Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680349Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680484Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680656Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680793Z do
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680932Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681071Z         in
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681222Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681375Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681566Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681703Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681838Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681972Z done
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682044Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682245Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682319Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682466Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682620Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682751Z then

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683032Z fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683103Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683824Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683974Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684188Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684363Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684496Z     #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684736Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684891Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685086Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685253Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685388Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685634Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685935Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686008Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686220Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686574Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686729Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686863Z       fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686996Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687070Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687238Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687547Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687621Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687815Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688102Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688324Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688475Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688634Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688768Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688953Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689086Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689161Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689343Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689489Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689622Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689839Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690152Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690383Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690522Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690662Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690734Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690904Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691069Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691290Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691425Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691561Z         fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691701Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691851Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691987Z     done
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692059Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692343Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692623Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692782Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693014Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693293Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693445Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693578Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693812Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694057Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694202Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694338Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694571Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694713Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694785Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695039Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695248Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695474Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695546Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695768Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695975Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696107Z     then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696435Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696646Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696784Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697001Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697073Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697272Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697469Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697637Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4709855Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710018Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710243Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710392Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710671Z fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710744Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710941Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711081Z strstr() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711223Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711353Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711425Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711578Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711713Z action() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711858Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711930Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712059Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712216Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712351Z     shift
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712488Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712642Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712775Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712923Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713058Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713189Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713329Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713463Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713601Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713674Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713882Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714034Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714246Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714385Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714540Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714684Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714850Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715002Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715205Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715363Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715551Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715702Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715903Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716056Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716233Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716568Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716648Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716803Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717023Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717397Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717473Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717621Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717765Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717914Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718052Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718191Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718334Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718516Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718657Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718797Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718931Z         ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4719067Z esac
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4719473Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4719630Z # Log that something succeeded

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720193Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720332Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720405Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720550Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720693Z failure() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720831Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720990Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721167Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721305Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721436Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721509Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721942Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722446Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722610Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722752Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722887Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723025Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723157Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723232Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723370Z echo_failure() {

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724564Z     return 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724698Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724905Z fscheck() {

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725181Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725319Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725468Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725608Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725739Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725880Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726039Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726170Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726308Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726724Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726878Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727010Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727141Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727316Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727500Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727630Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727762Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727912Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728069Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728200Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728331Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728481Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728632Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728764Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728897Z         *)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729100Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729373Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729511Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729643Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4745700Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4745842Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4745999Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746159Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746317Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746615Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746689Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746891Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747052Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747285Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747434Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747589Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747784Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748028Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748164Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748328Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748461Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748657Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748729Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748894Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749045Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749176Z     echo
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749386Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749637Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749787Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749919Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750126Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750334Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750580Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750651Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750795Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750966Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4751163Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4751372Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4751546Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4751712Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4751870Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4752007Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4752078Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4752231Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4752381Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4752523Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4752712Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4754567Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4754752Z fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4754828Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4755048Z # if we have a persistent /home, then we want to go ahead and mount it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4755249Z { ! strstr "$cmdline" nopersistenthome && [ "$homedev" ]; } &&
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4755443Z   action "Mounting persistent /home" mountPersistentHome
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4755519Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4470619Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4472181Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4472428Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4472884Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4473380Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4473991Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4474452Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4474967Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4475409Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4475751Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4476204Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4476752Z             #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4477012Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4477429Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4477868Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4478292Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4478746Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479090Z             #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479441Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479778Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4479982Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4480422Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4480866Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4481384Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4481649Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4481821Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4482149Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4482454Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4483017Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4483434Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4483781Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4484098Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4484479Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4484887Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4485330Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4485733Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4486091Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4486641Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487084Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487397Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487580Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4487840Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4504024Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4504291Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4504849Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4505447Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4508644Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4511300Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4517423Z             options,
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4517696Z         )

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4518458Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519219Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519382Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519725Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4519978Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4520218Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4520672Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4520950Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4521182Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4521565Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4521844Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4522187Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4522509Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4522752Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4523055Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4529960Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4530362Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4530510Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4530819Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4531182Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4531487Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4531755Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4532112Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4532512Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4532818Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533011Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533154Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533440Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4533875Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4534208Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4534452Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4534865Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535067Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535402Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535723Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4535993Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4536267Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4536656Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4536851Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537114Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537345Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537721Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4537994Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4538444Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4538853Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539032Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539180Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539413Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539599Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4539792Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4540156Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4540595Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541021Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541275Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541499Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4541878Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4542329Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4542668Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4542915Z         """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543189Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543503Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543682Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4543847Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4544373Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4544796Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4544986Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4545133Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4545441Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4545781Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4546078Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4546768Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547026Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547171Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547466Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4547861Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4548204Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4548495Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4548755Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4549112Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4549493Z             return False

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659021Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659196Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659461Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4659642Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4662621Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4662809Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4662950Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663254Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663394Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663558Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663768Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4663980Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664142Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664212Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664427Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664583Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664760Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4664937Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665135Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665279Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665462Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665606Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665739Z     """
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4665884Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666147Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666296Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666649Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4666823Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667085Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667241Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667457Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667644Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667717Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4667876Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668145Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668295Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668365Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668517Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668796Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4668873Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669018Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669089Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669160Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669390Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669530Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669723Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4669979Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670128Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670271Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670532Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670831Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4670980Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4671053Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4671202Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4671275Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4677896Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678127Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678353Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678574Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678705Z #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4678841Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679072Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679293Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679485Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679714Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679849Z #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4679982Z set -eu
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680202Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680347Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680483Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680654Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680791Z do
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4680931Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681069Z         in
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681220Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681372Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681564Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681701Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681836Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4681970Z done
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682042Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682240Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682317Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682464Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682618Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682750Z then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4682896Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683030Z fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683102Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683422Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683582Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683822Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4683973Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684186Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684361Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684494Z     #
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684734Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4684887Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685083Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685252Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685387Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685632Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4685933Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686006Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686218Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686572Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686727Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686861Z       fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4686995Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687068Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687236Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687447Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687620Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4687813Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688098Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688322Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688473Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688633Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688767Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4688951Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689084Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689157Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689341Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689488Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689620Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4689837Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690149Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690379Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690520Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690660Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690733Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4690903Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691067Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691288Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691423Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691559Z         fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691697Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691849Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4691985Z     done
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692057Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692340Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692622Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4692778Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693013Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693291Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693443Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693577Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4693811Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694055Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694200Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694336Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694569Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694712Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4694784Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695036Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695244Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695470Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695544Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695766Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4695973Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696105Z     then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696433Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696644Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696783Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4696999Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697072Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697270Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697468Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697635Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697706Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697880Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4697946Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698157Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698365Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698429Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698651Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698719Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698826Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698927Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4698992Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4699085Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4699230Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4699360Z then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4699505Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4699644Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4699775Z else

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4700354Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4700544Z     # if we have netcat, use it for waiting until ws is up
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4700694Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4700842Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4701004Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4701144Z             sleep 0.5;

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4701420Z     else

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4701710Z         sleep 3
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4701841Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4701911Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4702321Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4702649Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710016Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710241Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710390Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710521Z fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710742Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4710940Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711079Z strstr() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711221Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711351Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711424Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711572Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711711Z action() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711856Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4711929Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712058Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712214Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712350Z     shift
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712487Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712640Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712774Z     else
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4712922Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713056Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713188Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713327Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713461Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713596Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713673Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4713880Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714032Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714244Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714384Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714538Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714683Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4714849Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715001Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715203Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715361Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715549Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715700Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4715901Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716051Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716231Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716565Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716646Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4716801Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717021Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717394Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717471Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717619Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717763Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4717909Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718050Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718189Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718332Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718515Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718655Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718795Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4718930Z         ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4719061Z esac
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4719469Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4719628Z # Log that something succeeded

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720192Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720329Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720403Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720548Z # Log that something failed

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4720988Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721165Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721301Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721434Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721507Z

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4721941Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722102Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722253Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722608Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722750Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4722886Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723023Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723155Z }
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723230Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4723368Z echo_failure() {

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724428Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724563Z     return 1
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724696Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4724901Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725042Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725177Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725317Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725467Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725606Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725737Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4725878Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726035Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726168Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726307Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726723Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4726876Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727009Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727140Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727315Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727498Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727629Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727761Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4727910Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728064Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728199Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728329Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728479Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728631Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728763Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4728895Z         *)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729098Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729371Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729509Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4729641Z }

2026-10-25T06:57:13.4745698Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4745838Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4745997Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746157Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746315Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746614Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746687Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4746889Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747048Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747283Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747433Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747587Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4747782Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748026Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748162Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748326Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748459Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748655Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748727Z
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4748892Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749043Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749174Z     echo
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749384Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749632Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749785Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4749917Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:57:13.4750124Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
